# 15s Short v2: exact frame plan

Approved second-by-second argument: Sonnet leads Terminal-Bench 4.0 (64 vs 57); Argon leads AutomationBench-AA (78 vs 71). Source Artificial Analysis 30 September 2026; Sonnet Max versus Argon High. Owner approved a comment VIDEO end card. Model logos are identification only, no endorsement. Full state for each of 450 frames follows.

450 unique frames. Time = frame / 30. Every number below is a resolved value, not an instruction to interpolate. Renderer reads these exact states.

## Per-frame states

The full machine-readable states are in `frames-v2.json`. Each frame below lists visible node IDs and exact [x,y,scale,rotation,opacity,progress] values. Values absent from a node use [0,0,1,0,1,1].
